# night16 — STILL-VANISHING at every atypical value

Measurements only.

These are the `P` from night15's 57 PERIODS-VANISHING survivors for which
EXACT-PRIM returns a verified primitive on **every component of every**
**atypical fibre**, so that every period of `eta = dy/P_x` over every cycle
of every atypical fibre vanishes exactly.  Together with night15's generic-
fibre screen this is the strongest necessary condition the campaign has.

The last section of `ATYPICAL.md` records the one row where that picture
changes for an unrelated reason: `808e52fdb1b6` is obstructed at its
GENERIC fibres, which night15 never sampled (it measured only c = 1 and
c = -1, both of which are atypical values of that P).
